# V4-Flash 固定选择 MoE 训练参考

一个真实配置MoE层的shared/routed专家、router分数归一化与输入联合VJP；histogram为声明条件，不是实测。

离散选择边界、辅助目标、量化逐值等价和完整训练峰值不在此账。

| model | deepseek-v4-flash |

| scenario.layer_id | 3 |
| scenario.routing | balanced |
| scenario.counts | unknown (null) |

| assumptions[0] | One actual-geometry Flash MoE layer. World size1 removes all_reduce; distributed communication and per-rank expert placement are outside this contract. |
| assumptions[1] | Fixed unique selections are a smooth local branch; hash layers still compute router scores and normalized weights. Topk/hash/bias update objectives are not silently differentiated or declared untrainable. |
| assumptions[2] | Source clips gate above +limit only and up to [-limit,+limit]. Saved masks select backward gradients; exact kink points are excluded from numerical fixtures. |
| assumptions[3] | Source route weight multiplies F-wide activation before w2. This position is preserved; no extra H-wide route multiplication or duplicate route VJP is counted. |
| assumptions[4] | Matrix each dX/dW is derived from its own shape, not inference FLOPs times3. Unvisited expert matrix work is zero while stored parameters remain present. |
| assumptions[5] | Save strategy retains both unweighted product for route derivative and weighted down input for w2 dW, preventing hidden recomputation. FP32 values/boolean masks are mathematical reference storage, not actual allocator peak. |
